## 読み取り専用データの取得範囲

Ghidraが定義した境界から先頭32バイトまで取得。型は推定値です。ポインタ、再配置、書込可能領域は取得理由を別記します。

## 読み取り専用データ

- 参照命令 [`008d0cbc`](#insn-008d0cbc)

~~~json
{
  "source": "008d0cbc",
  "target": "Stack[-0x60]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~
- 参照命令 [`008d0cc0`](#insn-008d0cc0)

~~~json
{
  "source": "008d0cc0",
  "target": "Stack[-0x50]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~
- 参照命令 [`008d0cc4`](#insn-008d0cc4)

~~~json
{
  "source": "008d0cc4",
  "target": "Stack[-0x40]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~

~~~json

  "source": "008d0cc8",
  "target": "Stack[-0x30]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~

  "source": "008d0ccc",
